```text
Based on the search results, here's what I found about the LIBERO-Goal dataset and how to download it:

## Main HuggingFace Repository

The LIBERO datasets are stored in a repository that includes libero_goal, with demonstrations of each task stored in HDF5 files. The main repository is located at `yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets` on HuggingFace.

## LIBERO-Goal Specific Repository

The libero_goal folder on HuggingFace contains 6.37 GB of data and includes individual HDF5 files for different tasks. You can access the LIBERO-Goal dataset directly at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets/tree/main/libero_goal

## Download Methods

You can download the dataset from HuggingFace using the command: `python benchmark_scripts/download_libero_datasets.py --use-huggingface`, which can also be combined with specific dataset selection for LIBERO-Goal.

## Additional Resources

The official LIBERO repository provides a download script with more details for programmatic downloading of the datasets.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

### Assistant

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Still hitting the XET bridge. The `HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1` is not working because the huggingface_hub library version on the server might not support this env var, or the dataset uses XET storage which cannot be bypassed.

Let me try a different approach: use wget to download files directly from HF. The HF download URLs follow a pattern:
`https://huggingface.co/datasets/yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets/resolve/main/libero_goal/&lt;filename>`

From the log I can see the files:
1. put_the_bowl_on_top_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5


2. open_the_middle_drawer_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5
3. put_the_bowl_on_the_plate_demo.hdf5
4. put_the_cream_cheese_in_the_bowl_demo.hdf5
5. open_the_top_drawer_and_put_the_bowl_inside_demo.hdf5
6. push_the_plate_to_the_front_of_the_stove_demo.hdf5
7. put_the_bowl_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5
8. put_the_wine_bottle_on_the_rack_demo.hdf5

I need to get the complete file list. The direct HuggingFace download is hitting issues with XET's content-addressable storage bridge, so I should try upgrading huggingface_hub or switch to wget with direct URLs instead of relying on the LIBERO package download method.
```

```json
  "server": "westd-19183",
  "command": "source /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null; mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_goal; nohup bash -c '\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_goal\nBASE=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets/resolve/main/libero_goal\"\nFILES=\"\nopen_the_middle_drawer_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\nopen_the_top_drawer_and_put_the_bowl_inside_demo.hdf5\npush_the_plate_to_the_front_of_the_stove_demo.hdf5\nput_the_bowl_on_the_plate_demo.hdf5\nput_the_bowl_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5\nput_the_bowl_on_top_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\nput_the_cream_cheese_in_the_bowl_demo.hdf5\nput_the_wine_bottle_on_the_rack_demo.hdf5\nput_the_wine_bottle_on_top_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\nturn_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5\n\"\nfor f in $FILES; do\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    echo \"SKIP $f (exists)\"\n  else\n    echo \"Downloading $f ...\"\n    wget -q --show-progress --timeout=120 --tries=3 -O \"$f.tmp\" \"$BASE/$f\" && mv \"$f.tmp\" \"$f\" && echo \"OK $f\" || echo \"FAIL $f\"\n  fi\ndone\necho \"ALL_DONE\"\nls -lh *.hdf5 2>/dev/null\n' > /tmp/libero_goal_download.log 2>&1 &\necho \"PID=$!\""
}
```

```text
Actually, let me look at the file names more carefully. The LIBERO-Goal tasks are:
1. open_the_middle_drawer_of_the_cabinet


2. open_the_top_drawer_and_put_the_bowl_inside

10. turn_on_the_stove

I notice these tasks lack the SCENE prefixes that appear in LIBERO-10 datasets. That's a significant distinction—LIBERO-Goal is designed so all 10 tasks operate within the same visual scene but with different objectives, which is fundamentally different from how LIBERO-10 structures its scenes. }
```

```json
{
  "server": "westd-19183",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/download_libero_goal.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport requests\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nBASE_URL = \"https://huggingface.co/datasets/yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets/resolve/main/libero_goal\"\nOUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_goal\"\nFILES = [\n    \"open_the_middle_drawer_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"open_the_top_drawer_and_put_the_bowl_inside_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"push_the_plate_to_the_front_of_the_stove_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_bowl_on_the_plate_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_bowl_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_bowl_on_top_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_cream_cheese_in_the_bowl_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_wine_bottle_on_the_rack_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"put_the_wine_bottle_on_top_of_the_cabinet_demo.hdf5\",\n    \"turn_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5\",\n]\n\ndef download_file(fname, max_retries=5):\n    url = f\"{BASE_URL}/{fname}\"\n    out_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, fname)\n    tmp_path = out_path + \".part\"\n    \n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        try:\n            # Resume support\n            downloaded = 0\n            if os.path.exists(tmp_path):\n                downloaded = os.path.getsize(tmp_path)\n            \n            headers = {}\n            if downloaded > 0:\n                headers[\"Range\"] = f\"bytes={downloaded}-\"\n            \n            resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, stream=True, timeout=60, \n                              allow_redirects=True, verify=True)\n            \n            if resp.status_code == 416:  # Range not satisfiable = already complete\n                os.rename(tmp_path, out_path)\n                return f\"OK {fname} (already complete)\"\n            \n            if resp.status_code not in (200, 206):\n                print(f\"  [{fname}] HTTP {resp.status_code}, retry {attempt+1}\", flush=True)\n                time.sleep(2 ** attempt)\n                continue\n            \n            total = resp.headers.get(\"Content-Length\")\n            if total:\n                total = int(total) + downloaded\n            \n            mode = \"ab\" if resp.status_code == 206 else \"wb\"\n            if resp.status_code == 200:\n                downloaded = 0\n            \n            with open(tmp_path, mode) as f:\n                for chunk in resp.iter_content(chunk_size=1024*1024):  # 1MB chunks\n                    f.write(chunk)\n                    downloaded += len(chunk)\n            \n            # Verify size\n            actual_size = os.path.getsize(tmp_path)\n            if total and actual_size < total * 0.99:\n                print(f\"  [{fname}] Incomplete: {actual_size}/{total}, retry {attempt+1}\", flush=True)\n                time.sleep(2 ** attempt)\n                continue\n            \n            os.rename(tmp_path, out_path)\n            size_mb = actual_size / 1024**2\n            return f\"OK {fname} ({size_mb:.0f}MB)\"\n            \n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  [{fname}] Error: {e}, retry {attempt+1}\", flush=True)\n            time.sleep(2 ** attempt)\n    \n    return f\"FAIL {fname} after {max_retries} retries\"\n\nos.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\nprint(f\"Downloading {len(FILES)} files to {OUT_DIR}\", flush=True)\n\nt0 = time.time()\nwith ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:\n    futures = {pool.submit(download_file, f): f for f in FILES}\n    for fut in as_completed(futures):\n        print(fut.result(), flush=True)\n\nelapsed = time.time() - t0\nprint(f\"\\nDone in {elapsed/60:.1f} min\", flush=True)\n\n# Summary\nfor f in FILES:\n    p = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f)\n    if os.path.exists(p):\n        print(f\"  {f}: {os.path.getsize(p)/1024**2:.0f}MB\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"  {f}: MISSING\")\nPYEOF\necho \"Script created\""
}
```
